# Skills · V · part 39

776 entries, viva-harden-investigation to vlop-quellenkarte. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **viva-harden-investigation**: Use when an existing viva Investigation or Study needs to be made more rigorous, trustworthy, or defensible — an overclaiming verdict, a failing/partial report-card gate, a deferred/scaffolded study, an unresolved decisions_needed, a single-seed/uncalibrated claim, findings lacking biological interpretation, or a reviewer asking "is this real?"/"what does this mean biologically?". Also triggers on "harden", "de-risk", "stress-test", "make rigorous", or biology-forward authoring. ([vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers](https://github.com/vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers/tree/HEAD/skills/viva-harden-investigation))
- **viva-init**: Use when setting up a machine so Claude can invoke the /viva-* skills — a one-shot per-machine install of the skills into ~/.claude/skills/. ([vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers](https://github.com/vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers/tree/HEAD/skills/viva-init))
- **viva-investigation**: Use when grouping multiple Studies under a shared research question — creating, opening, listing, or closing an Investigation, or adding/removing member studies and orchestrating a run across all of them. ([vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers](https://github.com/vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers/tree/HEAD/skills/viva-investigation))
- **viva-model-build**: Use when an open-ended study question should be turned into a validated model by an autonomous loop — authors acceptance-criteria Tests, audits their sufficiency, SELECTs where the model comes from (reuse / compose / build-new, under a sourcing audit), locks them, then builds/runs/evaluates and iterates the MODEL (never the locked Tests) until the severity gate passes or gives up honestly. Drives the agentic model-building protocol. ([vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers](https://github.com/vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers/tree/HEAD/skills/viva-model-build))
- **viva-navigate**: Use when you need read-only answers about the workspace knowledge graph without running anything — the AC→study gating matrix and unlinked-AC gaps, which studies cite a source, which findings measure an observable, a study's prerequisite DAG, the ranked "decisions needed" list for an investigation, or a quick workspace/server/git status check. ([vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers](https://github.com/vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers/tree/HEAD/skills/viva-navigate))
- **viva-orient**: Use when starting any viva-superpowers session or workspace task — orients you to the /viva-* skills, the one automated arc from a research question to generated, gated study content, and the two preconditions every skill assumes. Auto-injected at session start; not a command to run. ([vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers](https://github.com/vivarium-collective/viva-superpowers/tree/HEAD/skills/viva-orient))
- **viva-prep**: Prepare and conduct an evidence-based oral defense or project viva using the student's own code, report, rubric, and runtime behavior. Use when asked to prepare for my viva, what will my examiners ask, help me summarise my thesis, I am worried about my methods chapter in the defence, or make a viva revision plan.
